GOVERNMENT OF PUNJAB
DEPARTMENT OF RURAL DEVELOPMENT AND PANCHAYATS
NOTIFICATION
The 8th January, 2019
No. PB./GP/P/Ludhiana/2019/5776.- In pursuance of provisions of Section 13 (1) of the Punjab
Panchayati Raj Act, 1994 (Punjab Act No. 9 of 1994) and all other powers enabling him in this behalf, the
Governor of Punjab do hereby notify the persons elected as Panches of Gram Panchayats mentioned against
them Column No. 5 comprising in the Blocks Dehlon, Doraha, Jagraon, Khanna, Ludhiana-1, Ludhiana-2,
Machhiwara, Maloudh, Pakhowal, Raikot, Samrala, Sidwan Bet and Sudhar of the District Ludhiana as per
schedule given below:-
